<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.396.1">“I’m not taking Hashimoto’s side in this, but what made you think that he wouldn’t be defended the second time?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.396.2">asked Kitou.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.397.1">“The logic is the same as it was in the first round. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.397.2">You can make up to two mistakes, so there’s no need for Horikita-san to defend him. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.397.3">If she were to, she’d want to leave that until the last possible minute. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.397.4">However, I imagine she would prefer Kouenji-kun to answer correctly.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.398.1">“…I see. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.398.2">Which means that now Horikita has no other choice but to protect Kouenji, even if she doesn’t want to,” muttered Kitou, now convinced.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.399.1">As long as there was room for Kouenji to make a mistake, Horikita would not allocate a protection slot for him. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.399.2">In other words, Sakayanagi took a risk and targeted him so that slot would be occupied in the later turns. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.399.3">That’s how Kitou had interpreted what she said. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.399.4">The fact that the problems in the gastronomy category were easy weren’t really a problem. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.399.5">Every class was feeling out the difficulty of the problems.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.400.1">“Sorry I doubted you, Princess. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.400.2">You really did think it through. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.400.3">If that’s what you were going to do, though, you could’ve gone after Kouenji starting from the first turn, right? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.400.4">You could have made them waste their protection slot for eight turns. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.400.5">We basically lost a turn, then,” said Hashimoto.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.401.1">“I was ninety-nine percent certain that she would not defend Kouenji-kun, but the reason I chose to target him on turn two was to ensure their complacency. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.401.2">It was a strategic opening move to lure our opponents into making a second blunder. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.401.3">What would have happened had I made my attack on turn one and Horikita-san had decided to defend him? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.401.4">It would have been harder going.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.402.1">They would have run the risk of being led around by the nose by their opponent, by a deceptive protection slot. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.402.2">Sakayanagi didn’t want to give her opponent any leeway to control the pace.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.403.1">“He’d answered an easy question incorrectly the first time, so I was able to determine he’d be unlikely to answer the second correctly. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.403.2">At any rate, the results were superb. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.403.3">All thanks to the information you have provided me.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.404.1">Hashimoto, feeling relieved that Sakayanagi was making effective use of his information, nodded and took his seat.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.405.1">“Now then, let’s finish off Kouenji-kun, shall we?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.405.2">said Sakayanagi.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.406.1">On the fourth turn, Sakayanagi nominated Kouenji for a third time. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.406.2">Again, he was the first person in her list of nominees. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.406.3">It shocked the class.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.407.1">“It is to make doubly sure, you see. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.407.2">This is a threat that I will go after him again if they show me an opening. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.407.3">Thanks to your gathered intelligence, Hashimoto-kun, we know the inner workings of Horikita-san’s class. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.407.4">However, our opponent does not know how much has been leaked to you, Hashimoto-kun.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.408.1">“I got it… You’re right. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.408.2">They’ll feel they have to protect him,” said Hashimoto.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.409.1">Sakayanagi chose the same category, gastronomy, but she raised the difficulty to 2. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.409.2">She was also checking to see what the increase in difficulty for the problems would be like. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.409.3">Although it’d be a waste if it turned out Kouenji were protected, he didn’t dare point that out. </span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.410.1">However, one last surprise was in store for them.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.411.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span style=" font-weight: bold; font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.412.1">Successful protections by the defender: </span></span><span style=" font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.413.1">Shinohara Satsuki, Sudou Ken</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.414.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.415.1">Horikita did the unimaginable: She’d chosen not to defend Kouenji.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.416.1">“Why isn’t she defending him?!” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.416.2">balked Hashimoto.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.417.1">“Are you sure you didn’t get bad information?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.417.2">asked Kamuro.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.418.1">“No! </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.418.2">I’m sure of it!” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.418.3">shouted Hashimoto.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.419.1">Kouenji had given the correct answer himself and thus avoided elimination. </span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.420.1">Hashimoto was confused; his head swam. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.420.2">Sakayanagi, on the other hand, looked as if her grasp on the situation had grown firmer after observing Horikita and Kouenji’s actions.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.421.1">“Does this mean that Horikita’s abandoning Kouenji…?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.421.2">asked Kamuro.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.422.1">“That’s an opening. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.422.2">We can crush him in one go,” said Kitou. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.422.3">He smelled blood in the water.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.423.1">“You may be right. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.423.2">And I’m sure trust in Horikita is dropping, along with their morale,” said Hashimoto.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.424.1">But while Hashimoto saw Horikita’s decision not to use her protection slot on Kouenji as a sign of internal strife in the enemy class, Sakayanagi had come to a different conclusion.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.425.1">“I thought she might protect him unconditionally or that this was our shot at eliminating him, but it seems Horikita-san has something else in mind. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.425.2">If we target Kouenji-kun any further, we will only end up playing into her hands.” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.425.3">With a giggle, Sakayanagi opened her phone. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.425.4">“At any rate, I’m impressed. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.425.5">It appears you’ve actually kept your opponent in mind.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.426.1">Sakayanagi wondered silently if Ayanokouji was lurking in Horikita’s shadow. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.426.2">Which one had come up with this strategy? </span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.427.1">“I’d guess this wasn’t his doing,” said Sakayanagi. </span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.428.1">If Ayanokouji were pulling the strings, she’d have heard about it. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.428.2">Sakayanagi would have felt the influence of some alien mind. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.428.3">She only detected the faint whispers of Ayanokouji in Horikita’s thinking.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.429.1">She’s been watching him more closely than anyone, so I’m sure she’s grown</span></span><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.430.1">, thought Sakayanagi.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.431.1">The lines were drawn. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.431.2">Sakayanagi was not going to fall behind Horikita in this battle.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.432.1">“The problem is…”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.433.1">For Sakayanagi, who held Class A’s future in her hands, there wasn’t any class in particular that worried her. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.433.2">More than one working in collusion, however, would be a problem—that was Sakayanagi’s sole concern. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.433.3">Although Sakayanagi’s agents continued to perform reconnaissance after the special exam was announced, there had been no signs of any such moves from the other classes. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.433.4">However, perhaps they’d been working in secret.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.434.1">All Sakayanagi could do was look for signs of that kind of an arrangement between classes. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.434.2">As of now, the likelihood that the other classes were working together was nearly zero. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.434.3">Nothing was out of the ordinary.</span></span></p>
</div>
</div>

  </section>

<div xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" id="book-columns"><div xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" id="book-inner">
<p id="auto_bookmark_toc_20" class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.1.1">“Well then, shall we take first place?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.1.2">said Sakayanagi.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.2.1">At the end of the first half, Sakayanagi had scored twenty-nine points. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.2.2">Having the lead was gratifying; however, looming right behind them was the specter of Class B, trailing by just a single point. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.2.3">Hashimoto forgot to even get up from his seat. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.2.4">He just stared at the results up on the monitor during their break.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.3.1">“Masumi-san, would you care to join me for lunch? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.3.2">I trust that since you have been eliminated already, you have time,” asked Sakayanagi.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.4.1">“Wow, you really don’t pull your punches, do you?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.4.2">said Kamuro.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.5.1">Sakayanagi smiled as if it were a compliment and leaned on her cane as she walked away. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.5.2">When the two girls stepped out into the hallway, Kitou silently followed.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.6.1">“Wait, she invited you too?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.6.2">asked Kamuro.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.7.1">“She texted me,” said Kitou.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.8.1">“Hm. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.8.2">So, are we okay with leaving Hashimoto out?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.8.3">asked Kamuro.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.9.1">Whenever Kamuro and Kitou were together, Hashimoto was almost always with them—Kamuro seemed concerned that he wasn’t.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.10.1">“He, regrettably, declined. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.10.2">He had been targeted by Ryuuen-kun and had answered incorrectly twice, after all. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.10.3">It is only natural that he doesn’t want to be eliminated,” replied Sakayanagi.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.11.1">A tiny, wry grin appeared on Kamuro’s face as she imagined Hashimoto frantically searching for information on his phone.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.12.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P__STAR__STAR__STAR__page_break"><span><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.13.1"> 7.4</span></span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.14.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span style=" font-weight: bold; font-size: 2.40em; letter-spacing: 0.60mm;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.15.1">A</span></span><span style=" font-size: 0.80em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.16.1">LTHOUGH THE CAFETERIA </span></span><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.17.1">was usually crowded, packed full of students from the entire school, it was deserted today. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.17.2">This was because many of the second-year students remained in their classroom, staring intently at their phones, like Kei was doing right now. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.17.3">Desperate to avoid elimination, they were poring over their review material on their phones. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.17.4">The only students who could afford to come to the cafeteria were the leaders or the students who had already been eliminated and had nothing left to do.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.18.1">Horikita and I both decided what to eat, made our purchases, and then carried our trays to the usual spot favored by second-year students. </span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.19.1">“Looks like we have our choice of seats today,” I remarked.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.20.1">“Sure does. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.20.2">But it’s strange, isn’t it? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.20.3">You would think that the first- and third-year students would be here at least,” said Horikita.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.21.1">It wasn’t like there was assigned seating for the classes. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.21.2">The students had, for some reason or another, drawn those lines on their own and followed them by unspoken agreement. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.21.3">Of course, some hardly cared about that sort of thing.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.22.1">“You don’t sweat the details, do you?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.22.2">I observed.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.23.1">“As if you do,” she snapped.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.24.1">“I’m a conformist, actually.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.25.1">“Ha. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.25.2">As if. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.25.3">But I don’t have the time to devote my mental resources to thinking about you right now,” said Horikita.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.26.1">That felt a little mean-spirited, but hey, whatever made her stop, I supposed. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.26.2">I was just about to break apart my pair of disposable chopsticks when I heard someone call out to us.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.27.1">“Good day to you, Class B students. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.27.2">May I join you?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.27.3">asked Sakayanagi. </span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.28.1">“Sit wherever you like. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.28.2">I’m not your boss,” said Horikita.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.29.1">Even though Horikita gave her permission, she was probably surprised. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.29.2">It was unusual for the enemy to want to hang out in the middle of a special exam.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.30.1">“I don’t mind either, but what about your food?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.30.2">I asked.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.31.1">Sakayanagi had arrived empty-handed. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.31.2">If she went to go buy food now, it’d take some time.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.32.1">“Masumi-san and Kitou-kun are making the purchase. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.32.2">They should be by shortly.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.33.1">At the end of my line of sight, I did indeed spot Kamuro and Kitou from behind as they were listlessly waiting in line.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.34.1">“They must really value you as a friend,” I remarked.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.35.1">“Yes. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.35.2">They are so helpful,” said Sakayanagi.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.36.1">As Sakayanagi sat down across from Horikita and propped up her cane, Kitou came over, dexterously balancing a tray in each hand. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.36.2">He was always there to support Sakayanagi.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.37.1">“Please sit down, both of you,” said Sakayanagi.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.38.1">“Huh? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.38.2">Eat with Horikita and Ayanokouji? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.38.3">I don’t really feel like it,” said Kamuro.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.39.1">“What’s the harm? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.39.2">It might be useful for you, Masumi-san, for future reference,” said Sakayanagi.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.40.1">“Are you trying to get me involved in your nonsense again? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.40.2">I’m really sick of these games,” said Kamuro.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.41.1">Kamuro’s name appeared on the list of eliminations from Class A, but she showed no signs of worry. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.41.2">While she did have a side to her that rebelled against Sakayanagi, her attitude was one that was only possible because of her absolute faith that Sakayanagi could not lose. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.41.3">The fact they were in first place certainly validated her conviction.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.42.1">I offered a casual greeting to Kitou, lightly raising my hand to say hello. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.42.2">Kitou didn’t react particularly strongly to that, but he inclined his head, which was enough.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.43.1">“Please go easy on me in the second half, Horikita-san,” said Sakayanagi.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.44.1">“You’re honestly asking me that? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.44.2">You went extremely hard on me in the first half</span></span><span class="C_Current__And__Properties__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.45.1">,</span></span><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.46.1">” said Horikita.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.47.1">“Oh, I was just playing with you. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.47.2">You’re in second place—isn’t that proof I’ve gone easy on you?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.47.3">said Sakayanagi.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.48.1">“You must be joking,” said Horikita, irritated by the implication that success was due to Sakayanagi’s grace.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.49.1">“Hey, lemme in on this too.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.50.1">Kitou, sensing the presence of this new arrival, immediately reeled around like he was under attack. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.50.2">This person didn’t seem bothered in the slightest and sat down next to Horikita before bothering to ask for permission.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.51.1">“A rowdy entrance, Ryuuen-kun,” said Sakayanagi.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.52.1">“Hah hah! </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.52.2">Well, the sheep seemed to be flockin’ together, so the wolf came to check on ’em,” said Ryuuen.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.53.1">He seemed awfully composed considering he was losing. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.53.2">That being said, there was no way he was up to anything, considering how worn out he looked.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.54.1">“Leave,” snapped Kitou with a quiet authority.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.55.1">“Huh? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.55.2">Like hell you got the right to give </span></span><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.56.1">me</span></span><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.57.1"> orders. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.57.2">’Sides, the little midget over there didn’t say a word, now did she?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.57.3">said Ryuuen.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.58.1">“Say the word. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.58.2">I will remove him immediately,” said Kitou.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.59.1">Kitou, while looking to Sakayanagi for permission, stood up in anticipation of duking it out with Ryuuen. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.59.2">Now that Ryuuen had insulted Sakayanagi too, Kitou’s transition to battle mode was complete. </span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.60.1">“No need for worry, Kitou-kun. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.60.2">He simply came here because he’s hungry. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.60.3">We ought to welcome frail Mr. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.60.4">Wolf with open arms,” said Sakayanagi.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.61.1">“He doesn’t seem to have any food with him. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.61.2">Having your minions like Ishizaki run your errands for you?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.61.3">said Kitou.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.62.1">“No, not hungry for food, but rather points in this special exam. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.62.2">We have only just completed the first half, but he’s fallen so far behind,” said Sakayanagi.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.63.1">“You’re not wrong,” said Kitou.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.64.1">Three of the classes were currently locked in close competition, and Ryuuen’s trailed far behind. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.64.2">Sakayanagi’s words were meant as lighthearted teasing, the tension hung thick in the air. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.64.3">Kitou, seemingly comforted that violence wasn’t about to break out, quietly sat back down.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.65.1">“So, anyway, Kamuro. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.65.2">You sure seem pretty calm for someone who might be sent packing,” said Ryuuen.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.66.1">Kamuro was just about to bring a piece of deep-fried horse mackerel to her mouth, but her hands stopped midair, and she shot a glare back at Ryuuen.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.67.1">“You too, Kitou. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.67.2">Next time ya make a mistake, you’ll be joinin’ the others who got eliminated,” said Ryuuen.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.68.1">“At the moment, my class is in first place. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.68.2">You, on the other hand, are in last. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.68.3">Are you fit to be having this conversation?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.68.4">Sakayanagi immediately shot back.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.69.1">“Even if I come in last place, all I lose are small fries. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.69.2">In your case, well, Kamuro and Yamamura are candidates for expulsion. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.69.3">Hell, if Kitou and Hashimoto mess up, that number jumps up to four of your people. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.69.4">Whoever disappears, you’re the one who is gonna be hurtin’. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.69.5">Maybe Horikita will painfully wreck your shit and eliminate even more people from your class, increasin’ that number with garbage people that no one cares about?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.69.6">said Ryuuen.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.70.1">A statement like </span></span><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.71.1">“We will have a few more eliminations, yes,” </span></span><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.72.1">was not something you would expect from Sakayanagi. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.72.2">For each person eliminated, you lost a point. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.72.3">Nobody wanted that.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.73.1">“So is your intention to have someone close to me eliminated?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.73.2">asked Sakayanagi.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.74.1">“Too late for me to bother explainin’ that to ya now,” said Ryuuen.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.75.1">“I find it hard to believe that would happen. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.75.2">I can only say this: your attempt to target students within your reach has utterly failed. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.75.3">You went after students like Masumi-san and Kitou-kun to upset me, and you can see where that’s gotten you,” said Sakayanagi. </span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.76.1">I got the strong impression that Ryuuen’s strategy was to target a maximum of eight people or so, those who supported Sakayanagi: people like Kitou, Kamuro, and Hashimoto. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.76.2">Out of the four classes, Sakayanagi had the highest rate of successful protections in the first half. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.76.3">Despite this weak attack, Sakayanagi could not protect Kamuro and Yamamura. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.76.4">Even if you knew what someone was aiming for, that didn’t mean you could defend against it. </span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.77.1">“Thanks to your infantile strategy, our class has managed to hold onto first place. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.77.2">I am grateful to you, though at the same time, I am worried for you, Ryuuen-kun. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.77.3">If you do not change the way you fight in the second half, you will simply lose. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.77.4">I trust you agree, yes, Horikita-san?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.77.5">asked Sakayanagi.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.78.1">“Yes, I certainly do think that Ryuuen-kun has overcommitted to a clumsy strategy. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.78.2">I would have scattered my attacks across more students once I realized that you tended to protect those students, Sakayanagi-san,” said Horikita.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.79.1">Ryuuen listened with a smile on his face, not having expected them to start discussing strategy.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.80.1">“I recommend that you fight smarter,” concluded Sakayanagi.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.81.1">Ryuuen didn’t cower. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.81.2">He rested his elbow on the table, like he was issuing a challenge.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.82.1">“Actually, I think I know ya pretty well, Sakayanagi. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.82.2">Go ahead, put the point difference issue aside for a hot minute. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.82.3">Think about it. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.82.4">Kamuro! </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.82.5">Say, hypothetically, if your class were to come in last place, and ya still have just these same two eliminations ya got right now, what decision d’ya think Sakayanagi would make?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.82.6">asked Ryuuen.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.83.1">Kamuro’s hands stopped. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.83.2">She didn’t say a word in response, but she seemed mildly rattled. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.83.3">What kind of decision would Sakayanagi make if faced with choosing between the two of them? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.83.4">Even Horikita was listening closely. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.83.5">What was Sakayanagi’s criteria for determining who to expel? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.83.6">However, Sakayanagi’s chopsticks didn’t stop moving. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.83.7">She ate placidly.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.84.1">“Can’t answer? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.84.2">Actually, wait—ya don’t wanna, do ya? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.84.3">What d’ya think, Horikita?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.84.4">asked Ryuuen.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.85.1">“What do I think? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.85.2">Why did you go after Yamamura-san in the first place? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.85.3">It seems like you’ve narrowed down your targets, but she doesn’t seem like the ideal target, does she?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.85.4">asked Horikita.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.86.1">Sakayanagi had brought Kamuro with her here, but she hadn’t brought Yamamura. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.86.2">It was natural to assume that Kamuro was more special to Sakayanagi than Yamamura. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.86.3">All the other students Ryuuen targeted were prominent people with outstanding talents, but Sakayanagi and Yamamura had a special connection. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.86.4">There are students who have value in areas beyond just visible abilities, like you might see in the OAA.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.87.1">“Well, I’m sure all y’all don’t know, so listen up. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.87.2">Yamamura’s just as valuable to Sakayanagi as Kamuro. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.87.3">She really dotes on that girl somethin’ fierce, y’know?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.87.4">said Ryuuen.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.88.1">Ryuuen forced Yamamura into the conversation. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.88.2">Sakayanagi finally stopped eating.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.89.1">“If that’s your opinion, I wouldn’t change it,” said Sakayanagi plainly. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.89.2">She seemed to have no interest in telling him what to think.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.90.1">“Speaking as a third party, I can’t weigh in. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.90.2">All I can say is that both Kamuro-san and Yamamura-san are exceptional allies to Sakayanagi-san,” said Horikita.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.91.1">Even if Horikita were wrong, she didn’t want to influence Sakayanagi.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.92.1">“They’re both exceptional? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.92.2">Hah! </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.92.3">Don’t make me laugh. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.92.4">There ain’t no way Sakayanagi would evaluate ’em based on shit like OAA ability. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.92.5">She only cares ’bout people as tools.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.93.1">“Huh.” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.93.2">Kamuro looked over at Sakayanagi and quietly sought reassurance. </span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.94.1">“Oh, so Yamamura’s name was a surprise for Kamuro here, eh?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.94.2">said Ryuuen.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.95.1">Ryuuen didn’t know everything that went on inside of Class A, either. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.95.2">This had nothing to do with the perception of or the relationship between Kamuro and Yamamura. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.95.3">He was simply provoking them. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.95.4">Even so, his words had real bite.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.96.1">“Are you and Yamamura close?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.96.2">asked Kamuro.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.97.1">“This is just flailing on Ryuuen-kun’s part,” said Sakayanagi.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.98.1">“I’m only asking because I didn’t know you and Yamamura had a connection,” said Kamuro.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.99.1">Kamuro paused slightly, but I wondered who else noticed.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.100.1">“I’ve already told you, haven’t I? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.100.2">He’s simply trying to provoke us. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.100.3">Ignore him,” said Sakayanagi.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.101.1">It wasn’t that she was avoiding the topic; she just honestly thought it was a waste of time. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.101.2">Though Ryuuen had stopped, he seemed to be enjoying watching Kamuro react to his words in a hyper-sensitive way despite his usual strength.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.102.1">“Go ahead and figure out which one yer gonna expel now, while ya still got time,” said Ryuuen.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.103.1">It would seem that Ryuuen’s appearance here wasn’t intended as a provocation against Sakayanagi. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.103.2">Rather, his purpose was to imprint a message that the goal was not to increase the number of eliminations in Class A any further. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.103.3">He only wanted to make a few people, like Kitou and Hashimoto, to face expulsion. </span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.104.1">“I hope you’re not swayed by so little,” said Sakayanagi, appealing to her not to adopt Ryuuen’s strategy.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.105.1">“No worries,” replied Horikita.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.106.1">Horikita was fighting to win. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.106.2">She wasn’t tackling this exam with the intention of expelling someone from Sakayanagi’s class. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.106.3">Unless she decided that was an effective way for her to win, of course. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.106.4">That would be a different story.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.107.1">Perhaps deciding that continued cheap provocations wouldn’t yield him any further gains, Ryuuen changed the topic of conversation. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.107.2">“Looks like the only one who didn’t come here is Ichinose, huh?”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.108.1">“Her class seems to have a clear policy of not letting anyone be eliminated. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.108.2">When I looked around the cafeteria, I didn’t see anyone from her class. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.108.3">It’s consistent,” said Horikita.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.109.1">It was true—there weren’t any students from Ichinose’s class here in the cafeteria. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.109.2">Even before coming here, I didn’t see them around, except for the bare minimum necessary activities like stopping by the restroom. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.109.3">They got their food ready right away and weren’t wasting any time.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.110.1">“Heh, seems like she’s prepared to lose so she can protect her personal friends. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.110.2">Deep down, she’s just stupid,” said Ryuuen.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.111.1">Ichinose really would want to give everyone the best care, even the people who got eliminated in the other classes. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.111.2">However, her own class would suffer if she were to lose the fight. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.111.3">The angel would need to be a demon to protect her classmates.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.112.1">“She doesn’t back down from her principles, no matter the special exam. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.112.2">That is precisely why I have managed to keep her in third place, by exploiting her weakness.” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.112.3">Horikita’s chopsticks stopped as she reflected on the first half of the exam.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.113.1">“She’s gotta be sick in the head if she’s willing to go that far. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.113.2">If she keeps at it, she’ll be throwing away her protection slots until the very end. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.113.3">That’s a boon for you, huh, Ryuuen?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.113.4">said Kamuro.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.114.1">Ryuuen could attack Ichinose’s class the same way that Horikita did. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.114.2">Unless he increased the number of students in her class who got two questions wrong, he had a high chance of blowing through all her protection slots. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.114.3">Even if Ryuuen wasn’t getting points for his own class, he couldn’t avoid holding those above him back so that he could move up in the rankings.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.115.1">“I will be the one to attack your class next. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.115.2">Even if your success rate with protections increases as more people are eliminated, how many points will you be able to score?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.115.3">asked Sakayanagi.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.116.1">Just like how she had laid her trap against Kouenji, Sakayanagi anticipated her other opponent’s attack. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.116.2">Depending on how Ryuuen used his protection slots, his attacks might be wasted. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.116.3">It was especially difficult to protect allies who couldn’t protect themselves. </span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.117.1">Ryuuen violently shot up out of his chair. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.117.2">“Lookin’ forward to it.” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.117.3">He brushed his hair back with his palm as he turned to depart.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.118.1">“It appears that the disruption is leaving, so let us resume our meal,” said Sakayanagi.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.119.1">Contrary to what those around him thought, the look on his face contained hidden traces of a strong will. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.119.2">He had a plan. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.119.3">It probably was no mere coincidence I was the only one he’d shown that face to. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.119.4">The message he gave me was an intimidating one: “</span></span><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.120.1">Shut up and watch.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.121.1">Maybe he can turn this around. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.121.2">His weak act was just that—an act. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.121.3">Let’s see. </span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.122.1">The second half was about to begin.</span></span></p>
</div>
</div>
