  </section>

<div xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" id="book-columns"><div xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" id="book-inner">
<p id="auto_bookmark_toc_22" class="P__STAR__STAR__STAR__page_break"><span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.1.1">7.5</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.2.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span style=" font-weight: bold; font-size: 2.40em; letter-spacing: 0.60mm;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.3.1">I</span></span><span style=" font-size: 0.80em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.4.1">N JUST A FEW MINUTES,</span></span><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.5.1"> Sakagami-sensei would give us the signal to begin the eleventh turn. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.5.2">Horikita stood up on the podium and commanded the attention of the students.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.6.1">“Class A is a formidable opponent. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.6.2">They didn’t hand over first place once in the first half. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.6.3">Therefore, it’s important that we don’t get distracted. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.6.4">Ultimately, all we need to do is rack up enough points,” said Horikita.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.7.1">Horikita would be attacking Sakayanagi’s class, easily the most formidable. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.7.2">Sakayanagi’s class had many successful protections versus Ryuuen in the first half, and the students in her class also had a high percentage of questions answered correctly.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.8.1">“How are you going to attack?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.8.2">asked Sudou.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.9.1">In response to Sudou’s rather simple question, Horikita looked from one classmate to the next. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.9.2">Some people in this room might’ve been in communication with Sakayanagi—Horikita obviously couldn’t speak openly.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.10.1">“I’m sure you remember how I asked for everyone’s input when we were preparing for the exam. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.10.2">I’ve sorted through it all and chosen my strategy,” said Horikita.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.11.1">From the sound of it, she was going to target each student’s respective weaknesses instead of trying to out-wit Sakayanagi. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.11.2">However, my impression was that as little as we knew about Ichinose’s class, we knew even less about Sakayanagi’s class. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.11.3">They’d sealed their lips the moment the special exam was announced, so it hadn’t been an easy task to find their weaknesses.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.12.1">Only Horikita could sort through what information was useful and what was junk—that was basically what she was saying. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.12.2">It was time for Horikita’s first attack on Sakayanagi, on turn eleven. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.12.3">For her first move, she spent one point and chose the Literature category at a difficulty level of 2. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.12.4">Unfortunately, one student had been protected, but Sakayanagi’s class had only managed to score two points because three of the four students got the higher-difficulty question wrong.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.13.1">We had earned three points, but after taking away the one point we had used to increase the difficulty level, that made us even. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.13.2">If we scored four or more points, that would help us pull ahead. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.13.3">Horikita had applied a little pressure, but that was all. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.13.4">Then, on Sakayanagi’s turn, she suddenly spent two points, choosing the Sports category at difficulty level 3. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.13.5">It was a merciless assault on her opponent, Ryuuen, who was ranked last.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.14.1">“She’s determined to drive Ryuuen-kun into a corner… That’s quite confident of her,” said Horikita.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.15.1">Their start in the second half was quite different, with less concern about the point difference between the classes. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.15.2">Immediately afterward, though, there were cries of surprise from our class as they saw the results on the monitor.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.16.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span style=" font-weight: bold; font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.17.1">Successful protections by the defender: </span></span><span style=" font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.18.1">Katsuragi Kouhei, Shiina Hiyori, Tokitou Hiroya, Nomura Yuuji, Ibuki Mio</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.19.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.20.1">For the first time in this special exam, someone had gotten a perfect score with just their protection slots—five points in one go. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.20.2">A difficulty level of three would be meaningless if everyone were protected. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.20.3">What a painful blow for Sakayanagi. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.20.4">Ryuuen, who had been lagging behind the rest, suddenly jumped up to twenty-four points, putting him in line with Ichinose, at least temporarily.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.21.1">“This is strange,” said Horikita.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.22.1">Many had expected Ryuuen’s class to have continued to slip further and further away from the rest of the pack. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.22.2">Maybe they would keep this sudden momentum going, but the attack that followed against Ichinose’s class showed that their intuition wasn’t quite that good. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.22.3">Three students in Ichinose’s class were protected. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.22.4">However, one student in her class got the question wrong, so their class had only gotten four points, bringing them to twenty-eight. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.22.5">There was still our class’s defense left in this round, but Ichinose had closed the gap between us. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.22.6">Now that she caught up, what kind of category and which targets would she choose?</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.23.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span style=" font-weight: bold; font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.24.1">Category: </span></span><span style=" font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.25.1">Sports</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span style=" font-weight: bold; font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.26.1">Difficulty Level: </span></span><span style=" font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.27.1">1</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.28.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span style=" font-weight: bold; font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.29.1">Successful protections by the defender: </span></span><span style=" font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.30.1">Wang Mei-Yu, Shinohara Satsuki</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.31.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span style=" font-weight: bold; font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.32.1">Students nominated by the attacker: </span></span><span style=" font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.33.1">Ayanokouji Kiyotaka, Miyamoto Soushi, Karuizawa Kei</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.34.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.35.1">Among the attack nominations from Ichinose’s class, my name was listed first. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.35.2">And, whether intentionally or coincidentally, Kei’s name was also on the list. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.35.3">Her choice of attack, Sports at difficulty level 1, was exquisite. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.35.4">It wasn’t something that I could say I was particularly good or bad at. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.35.5">I was confident that I could get the answer right if the question was based on history or rules, but if it had to do with current events, then that would put me at a disadvantage. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.35.6">On the other hand, in Kei’s case, she might have seen a current event on TV. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.35.7">She’d mentioned that she often watched things like volleyball.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.36.1">“In a situation where there are fewer than two outs, there are runners on first and second or on first, second, and third, and a fly ball hit by the batter could be caught by an infielder with ordinary effort, what rule applies?” </span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.37.1">Fortunately, I was familiar with sports rules to some extent, so I was able to answer the question without any difficulty. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.37.2">The correct answer was the “infield fly rule.” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.37.3">However, Miyamoto aside, I couldn’t imagine that Kei would know this. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.37.4">I hoped that this would have somehow been among the things that Kei had managed to learn in these last few days of preparation, but…</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.38.1">Only me and Miyamoto answered correctly. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.38.2">Kei got it wrong. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.38.3">That being said, this was her first time faltering, so she had no reason to panic yet. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.38.4">However, as she returned to her seat, her face was creased with anxiety. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.38.5">Meanwhile, Miyamoto, who had answered correctly, rejoiced with Ike and his other friends, sharing high fives. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.38.6">From what I overheard from their chatter, he’d learned the answer from video games, and that’d come in handy today. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.38.7">You never knew when trivia would come in handy.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.39.1">This gave us four points. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.39.2">We’d overtaken Sakayanagi’s class, at least for the time being. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.39.3">It was time for the next turn, turn twelve. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.39.4">Sakayanagi’s class got four correct answers, earning four points and solidly increasing their score. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.39.5">However, the bigger shock was what Ryuuen’s class did. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.39.6">As if we were watching a replay of the last round, we once again saw five names on the list of successful protections. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.39.7">He had managed to get a perfect score with just protections twice in a row. </span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.40.1">“Dude, what are the chances of that?! </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.40.2">Do they just have, like, </span></span><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.41.1">crazy</span></span><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.42.1"> good luck or something?!” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.42.2">shouted Ike. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.42.3">He held his head in his hands at the shock of it.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.43.1">“…I wonder if we can really chalk it up to luck,” said Horikita.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.44.1">I sensed a heaviness in Horikita’s voice as she calmly stared at the monitor in her seat beside me. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.44.2">She was right. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.44.3">The odds of getting perfect scores twice in a row were quite low. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.44.4">If they got another perfect score next round, then…</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.45.1">While the feelings of shock lingered in our class, in the following attack on Ichinose’s class, two people were protected and two had answered questions correctly. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.45.2">The time for Ichinose’s next move had come.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.46.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span style=" font-weight: bold; font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.47.1">Successful protections by the defender: </span></span><span style=" font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.48.1">Ishikura Kayoko, Sudou Ken</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.49.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span style=" font-weight: bold; font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.50.1">Students nominated by attacker:</span></span><span style=" font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.51.1"> Ayanokouji Kiyotaka, Matsushita Chiaki, Karuizawa Kei</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.52.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.53.1">My and Kei’s names were among the nominations for the second time in a row. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.53.2">The instant Kei saw her name, she shot up out of her chair and, looking rather distraught, let out a shout.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.54.1">“She’s totally coming after me! </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.54.2">She is, isn’t she?!” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.54.3">wailed Kei.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.55.1">“Calm down. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.55.2">Just because you’ve been nominated twice in a row doesn’t mean that she’s concentrating on you,” said Horikita.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.56.1">“B-but—!”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.57.1">Her feelings were understandable. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.57.2">She probably would have understood if she felt as if she were a weak target. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.57.3">However, our opponent was Ichinose. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.57.4">Kei must have suspected that she was being targeted for personal reasons. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.57.5">Well, given that I was included among the nominations, the personal nature of the attack seemed pretty obvious, but in truth, whether it was personal or not, it was certainly meant to shake us.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.58.1">At any rate, it was a solid move. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.58.2">Horikita wasn’t going to use one of her precious protection slots on me. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.58.3">I’m sure that Ichinose must have read what Horikita was thinking there. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.58.4">If the category were something related to academics, there was a possibility that Kei would’ve been protected, but the category she chose was News. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.58.5">There was a good possibility that even Kei could solve a question in that category, so Horikita left Kei open. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.58.6">As for me, I’d already gotten a question wrong in this same category in the first half of the exam, so I needed to be careful.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.59.1">“What does ‘have a boba’ mean?”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.60.1">I froze.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.61.1">Wha? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.61.2">Huh? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.61.3">What? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.61.4">Have a boba? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.61.5">A boba…? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.61.6">Boba…?</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.62.1">The clock ran out on me, and I left my answer blank. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.62.2">Most questions in the News categories had been related to politics and recent events. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.62.3">Why were all my questions curveballs? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.62.4">The outcome for this mystery question was the opposite of last time: I got it wrong, and Kei got it right. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.62.5">Kei, relieved that she hadn’t been put in range of elimination, seemed to settle down immediately. </span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.63.1">Matsushita seemed to have answered correctly too, like it came naturally, and thus our class had secured four points. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.63.2">I, on the other hand, had made two mistakes. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.63.3">I was at risk. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.63.4">The right answer was “to have a drink that has tapioca balls in it.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.64.1">“Ugh, you… Do you know even </span></span><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.65.1">less</span></span><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.66.1"> about the world than I think you do?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.66.2">Horikita huffed, pointing her finger at me in apparent disgust after I had returned to my seat.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.67.1">I could only slump my shoulders.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.68.1">It was time for Horikita’s class’s attack on turn thirteen. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.68.2">The category was Kanji, with a difficulty level of 1. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.68.3">Unexpectedly, Horikita fell silent. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.68.4">Although she had smoothly rattled off the names of up to four nominations, she stumbled on the last one. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.68.5">It was an arduous task to keep all the information straight in her head.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.69.1">Who was good at what? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.69.2">Who was bad at what? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.69.3">The info on Class A was very limited. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.69.4">There was still time left. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.69.5">Horikita took a deep breath to try to calm herself down. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.69.6">Someone came to her rescue.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.70.1">“Satonaka’s a good choice,” muttered a single student in a bored, detached manner.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.71.1">That student was Kushida. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.71.2">She was well-rested, having not yet been nominated.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.72.1">“Thank you, Kushida-san. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.72.2">Sensei, please enter Satonaka-kun as our final nomination,” said Horikita.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.73.1">Without even asking Kushida the reason, Horikita took her advice; it was like she trusted her completely. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.73.2">And it paid off: Satonaka wasn’t protected, and he had also gotten the question wrong.</span></span></p>
</div>
</div>

<div xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" id="book-columns"><div xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" id="book-inner">
<p id="auto_bookmark_toc_24" class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.1.1">“Where’d you hear that Satonaka’s not good with kanji?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.1.2">asked Makita.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.2.1">Makita showered Kushida with admiration.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.3.1">“Where? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.3.2">Around. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.3.3">You just kinda hear stuff,” said Kushida. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.3.4">She looked off in a random direction, appearing disinterested.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.4.1">“You’ve been a major help. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.4.2">Thank you,” said Horikita.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.5.1">“Don’t mention it,” said Kushida.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.6.1">Horikita expressed her gratitude, but Kushida didn’t seem happy. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.6.2">Given her tenuous position in the class, it wasn’t a bad idea for her to make tangible contributions like this. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.6.3">Apparently, that was one of the reasons why Horikita felt confident going into the second half of the exam. </span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.7.1">Kushida Kikyou didn’t simply have a wide circle of friends; her hobby was to suss out weakness, and she had a memory for it. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.7.2">Because of that, she had built up an extraordinary information network. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.7.3">Although only one aspect of her information network had been demonstrated here, it was safe to assume that she had passed on a great deal to Horikita in advance. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.7.4">What an incredible asset.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.8.1">Turn thirteen: Ichinose’s attack. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.8.2">On our third round of defense against Ichinose, Horikita had chosen to defend Karuizawa. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.8.3">I, who was within range of being eliminated, was not protected, apparently abandoned. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.8.4">However, it would appear that Horikita’s read on the situation had been correct, and the name Karuizawa Kei appeared on the list of successful protections. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.8.5">Normally, someone would have been happy to dodge solving a problem, but Kei clearly wasn’t feeling great.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.9.1">“…She’s trying to eliminate me…! </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.9.2">No matter how you look at it, she’s coming after me, right?!”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.10.1">“Yeah, this is a little intense… Maybe,” replied Satou, sounding like she agreed with her. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.10.2">But entertaining strange assumptions would just amp up her anxiety to worry about it.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.11.1">“She’s not the type who would try to trap a particular person,” said Horikita.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.12.1">“Still!” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.12.2">wailed Kei.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.13.1">Kei looked like she was about to argue that Horikita was only saying that because she didn’t know what was going on, but she decided against it.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.14.1">“Anyway, while we can’t read her mind, now that I’ve successfully protected you, you probably won’t be targeted again,” said Horikita.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.15.1">“Okay…” said Kei.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.16.1">“Besides, when someone makes the same nomination three times in a row consecutively, like what happened with Kouenji-kun in the first place, it draws attention. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.16.2">I wonder what Ichinose-san is thinking,” said Horikita.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.17.1">The next turn began: Ichinose’s attack on turn fourteen.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.18.1">“…What should I do? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.18.2">I wonder,” said Horikita.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.19.1">Horikita seemed hesitant while allocating her protection slots. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.19.2">All things considered, there was no way that Kei would be nominated four times in a row. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.19.3">I was sure Horikita was wondering whether to go with that assumption, or if she should be wary that Ichinose would outsmart her.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.20.1">“I think you should try defending her again. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.20.2">Don’t you? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.20.3">I have a feeling Ichinose-san is after her,” said Kushida, offering advice to Horikita while she was thinking.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.21.1">“You really think so?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.21.2">asked Horikita.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.22.1">“Just my intuition based on her track record,” said Kushida.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.23.1">Kushida was basing her advice on Ichinose Honami’s thoughts and feelings rather than limiting her observations to the exam.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.24.1">“You’re right. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.24.2">It might be a wiser move to protect her once again,” said Horikita.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.25.1">Kei wasn’t in range of elimination yet, but Horikita wanted to be able to rack up a safe point if Ichinose was going to target her. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.25.2">The list of successful protections was revealed, and Kei’s name appeared among them once again. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.25.3">She had set a new record in this special exam, being nominated four times in a row, surpassing Kouenji’s three consecutive nominations. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.25.4">While the decision was baffling, it was great news that our class was able to compete with the other classes in an evenly matched battle.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.26.1">However, the situation started to deteriorate.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.27.1">While both Horikita and Sakayanagi’s respective classes were scoring points with a good balance between offense and defense, Ichinose’s class had a better hit rate at defense. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.27.2">However, Ryuuen’s class showed even more intense momentum. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.27.3">Sakayanagi had fought back with irregular categories and nominations time and time again, but she couldn’t change the situation for the better. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.27.4">Ryuuen had ended up getting four perfect turns in a row.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.28.1">There was no way this was luck. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.28.2">However, there was nothing that Horikita could do. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.28.3">Her only option was to rack up points and keep her cool.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.29.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P__STAR__STAR__STAR__page_break"><span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.30.1">7.6</span></span></p>

<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span style=" font-weight: bold; font-size: 2.40em; letter-spacing: 0.60mm;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.32.1">F</span></span><span style=" font-size: 0.80em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.33.1">OR RYUUEN, L</span></span><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.34.1">osing wasn’t a hardship. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.34.2">Even if he lost once, he’d just win the second time. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.34.3">Even if he lost a hundred times, it’d be fine as long as he won in the end. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.34.4">He lived his life thinking that way, but one day, a big obstacle stood in front of him. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.34.5">That guy hid a beast behind his idiot face. </span></span><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.35.1">Nah, that was way too weak a way of putting it,</span></span><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.36.1"> thought Ryuuen. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.36.2">The answer of how to best describe it escaped him.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.37.1">However, Ryuuen was sure that that guy was the most powerful, vicious human being he had ever met. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.37.2">He didn’t live in the realm of the attainable. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.37.3">It had been over a year since Ryuuen had been defeated by him—Ayanokouji—and had his spirit shattered to pieces. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.37.4">They were in different stratospheres. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.37.5">Perhaps that was why Ryuuen hardly felt any hatred welling up inside him.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.38.1">It was also true that, whenever he talked to Ayanokouji, he didn’t hate it. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.38.2">Perhaps it was because of this that, even if Ryuuen denied it on the surface, he… No, that wasn’t right. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.38.3">Deep down in his heart, Ryuuen acknowledged Ayanokouji’s outstanding abilities. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.38.4">That was all. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.38.5">But Ryuuen still told himself not to get the wrong idea.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.39.1">Ryuuen did not intend to bend the knee forever. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.39.2">Before Ayanokouji graduated from this school and they parted ways, Ryuuen was absolutely going to get his revenge. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.39.3">The first thing he needed to do was clear some junk out of his way. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.39.4">He’d decided to shut out Sakayanagi, the queen of Class A, keeping her from scoring. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.39.5">Sakayanagi was his only obstacle. </span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.40.1">Then he would defeat Ayanokouji.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.41.1">That was Ryuuen Kakeru’s goal while at this school. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.41.2">Until he did that, he would never, ever stop. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.41.3">Immediately after the second half of the exam commenced, Ryuuen, who had up until that point just been indifferently focusing his attacks on Sakayanagi’s chief forces turn after turn, rose from his seat.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.42.1">“Alrighty then… Well, guess it’s time I got this thing started. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.42.2">Outta my way,” barked Ryuuen.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.43.1">“Wh-what are—hey?!”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.44.1">After shoving the instructor in charge of their class, Hoshinomiya, out of his way, Ryuuen hoisted himself up to sit on the podium. </span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.45.1">“We’re in the second half. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.45.2">There’s only a ten-point gap. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.45.3">We can catch up with just a couple of perfect rounds. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.45.4">I can expect that from you, can’t I?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.45.5">ordered Ryuuen.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.46.1">The pressure the leader was exerting on his class was this: </span></span><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.47.1">I will not allow you to make any mistakes, no matter what task comes your way.</span></span><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.48.1"> Of course, if such threatening words were enough to increase the percentage of questions answered correctly, they wouldn’t have been in trouble in the first place.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.49.1">“Oh, piss off. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.49.2">We’re not the only class with that problem, yanno. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.49.3">You know damn well how hard it is to get a perfect score, even for the other classes, Ryuuen. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.49.4">If you’re the leader, then use your head and get the protections right,” snapped Tokitou, who was no coward.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.50.1">“Heh heh. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.50.2">You made two mistakes. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.50.3">Yer back’s up against the wall. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.50.4">You get eliminated, and punks like you will be joinin’ my top picks to get kicked out,” replied Ryuuen.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.51.1">“…Tch.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.52.1">“But don’t you worry. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.52.2">Startin’ now, I’m gonna give ya exactly what yer hopin’ for,” said Ryuuen.</span></span></p>
</div>
</div>
