<div xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" id="book-columns"><div xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" id="book-inner">
<p id="auto_bookmark_toc_26" class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.1.1">“What do you mean?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.1.2">asked Tokitou.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.2.1">“The part about me expecting perfection from all of you? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.2.2">That was a lie.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.3.1">Ryuuen looked over his shoulder and confirmed that the monitor had refreshed. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.3.2">There was a notification that indicated Sakayanagi had finished giving her nominations, and now it was Ryuuen’s turn to say how he would be assigning his five protection slots. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.3.3">The category that his opponent had chosen was Sports. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.3.4">Moreover, Sakayanagi had spent two points to set it at a difficulty level of 3.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.4.1">Sakayanagi wasn’t going to give Ryuuen’s class a single point. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.4.2">The students were left flustered in the face of such a merciless attack from Class A. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.4.3">Ryuuen alone, however, was chuckling to himself, gloating about his impending victory.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.5.1">“Katsuragi, Shiina, Tokitou, Nomura, and Ibuki. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.5.2">Hurry up and put ’em in,” barked Ryuuen.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.6.1">Almost as if to show that he didn’t care one whit about things like the category, the difficulty level, nothing like that, Ryuuen immediately gave his five names.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.7.1">“H-hey now, hold on just a minute here. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.7.2">Don’t start ordering teachers around, okay? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.7.3">Sheesh,” said Hoshinomiya.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.8.1">Though she was flustered, she went ahead and input the five names that Ryuuen had communicated to her, thus bringing the defense’s turn to an end. </span></span><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.9.1">By making his nominations without even thinking about it, was Ryuuen trying to avoid overthinking it?</span></span><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.10.1"> While some students were wondering, the results popped up.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.11.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span style=" font-weight: bold; font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.12.1">Successful protections by the defender: </span></span><span style=" font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.13.1">Katsuragi Kouhei, Shiina Hiyori, Tokitou Hiroya, Nomura Yuuji, Ibuki Mio</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.14.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.15.1">“Wh-what…?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.15.2">sputtered Tokitou.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.16.1">Tokitou, standing stock still as he glared at Ryuuen, was astonished. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.16.2">Perhaps Ryuuen’s immediate action without hesitation had paid off, because he had made good on his words and got a perfect score.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.17.1">“Gamblin’ is a good thing, y’know? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.17.2">You just roll the dice,” said Ryuuen.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.18.1">On the following turn, turn twelve, Ryuuen once again gave his five nominations without a second thought. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.18.2">Once again, everyone he nominated had been successfully protected. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.18.3">In a mere two turns, Ryuuen was in hot pursuit of first place. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.18.4">Turns thirteen and fourteen played out exactly the same. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.18.5">Even though Sakayanagi had spread out her nominations, Ryuuen struck them all down like he was sending out guided missiles, successfully executing his defense.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.19.1">“Heh heh</span></span><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.20.1">.</span></span><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.21.1"> Doesn’t seem like there’s anythin’ ya can do ’bout this, Sakayanagi,” boasted Ryuuen.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.22.1">Before this special exam began, Ryuuen was focused on something entirely different from the other leaders. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.22.2">Namely, to what extent could the scent of a beast be removed? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.22.3">How far could he corner his prey without letting them realize that he was directly behind them, champing at their heels? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.22.4">There was nothing the other classes could do about it even if they took steps now. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.22.5">Ryuuen had strategically launched his counterattack when the other leaders became complacent.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.23.1">“What on earth is going on…?!” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.23.2">wondered Hoshinomiya.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.24.1">This was some kind of calculated manipulation on Ryuuen’s part, but not even the instructor in charge of the class knew just what that trick was.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.25.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P__STAR__STAR__STAR__page_break"><span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.26.1">7.7</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.27.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span style=" font-weight: bold; font-size: 2.40em; letter-spacing: 0.60mm;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.28.1">I</span></span><span style=" font-size: 0.80em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.29.1">CHINOSE KEPT REPEATING</span></span><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.30.1"> the same words to her classmates.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.31.1">“I will never, ever let anyone from this class be expelled. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.31.2">Never doubt it.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.32.1">Even though her classmates believed in her, more than a few of them still felt uneasy. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.32.2">That was why Ichinose’s statement was so reassuring—she was choosing her words carefully. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.32.3">However, if she fought in the same way as she had before, the other classes would exploit that opening without mercy. </span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.33.1">The fundamental goal was to keep the number of eliminations at zero as a hedge against losing. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.33.2">If there weren’t any eliminations, there wouldn’t be any expulsions, even if they came in last place. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.33.3">It was a defensive strategy. </span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.34.1">However, Ichinose was not going to give up on winning. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.34.2">How was she going to fight to win while defending? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.34.3">Not by fighting in her opponent’s arena, but rather by dragging her opponent into hers. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.34.4">Ichinose was going to stand her ground and avoid expulsions, and that way, she would lure her opponent into complacency.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.35.1">As the exam progressed into turns two and three in the first half, Horikita’s aim had become clear. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.35.2">She was going after an unspecified large number of people, increasing the number of students in range of elimination by getting as many to two wrong answers as possible. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.35.3">When the number of people with two mistakes swelled to five, Horikita intended to assess what Ichinose would do.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.36.1">“Thank you, Horikita-san,” said Ichinose.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.37.1">Ichinose was truly grateful for Horikita’s wise but merciful actions. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.37.2">Horikita didn’t care about whether she eliminated students from her opponent’s class, as long as she could score points. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.37.3">It would’ve been a problem otherwise. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.37.4">Ichinose was fortunate that her first opponent hadn’t been Ryuuen, who was cruel and erratic, but Horikita, who attacked in a reliable, steady manner. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.37.5">Ichinose used her protection slots, giving top priority to the students in range of elimination.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.38.1">“I won’t abandon anyone. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.38.2">You believe that, right?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.38.3">asked Ichinose.</span></span></p>
</div>
</div>

<section epub:type="frontmatter titlepage">
    <img alt="Front Image1" class="insert" src="/hola-lector-agua/images/books/y2v10/OEBPS/Images/INTERIORIMAGES_06.jpg" loading="lazy" decoding="async"/>
  </section>

<div xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" id="book-columns"><div xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" id="book-inner">
<p id="auto_bookmark_toc_28" class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.1.1">Ichinose’s dearest wish was to protect her friends. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.1.2">She calmed them so that they wouldn’t make any reckless moves.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.2.1">“It would be best if no one has to be expelled…not just from our class or grade, but from our school as a whole,” said Ichinose.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.3.1">There was nothing false about that feeling she expressed. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.3.2">However, if sacrifices were required to protect her class, then she would spare no hesitation in eliminating students in Ryuuen’s class—to win, someone had to lose. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.3.3">As a result, four people from Ryuuen’s class had been eliminated by the end of the first half through Ichinose’s attacks. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.3.4">If someone were to disappear from those four in the end, that meant she would be responsible for their expulsion, albeit indirectly. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.3.5">Unavoidable casualties. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.3.6">Though they’d be heartbroken, there would be no other choice.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.4.1">…However, that was only the case if Ryuuen lost.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.5.1">“The second half will begin in one minute. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.5.2">Everyone, take your seats and get ready,” announced Mashima-sensei.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.6.1">After receiving that instruction from Mashima-sensei, Ichinose took out her phone. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.6.2">She slowly looked through her chat history in the messenger app, at a conversation with a certain person. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.6.3">It was from right after the first half had started.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.7.1">“Ryuuen-kun, I know this is sudden, but would you like to cooperate with me? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.7.2">I do not want anyone to get expelled from my class, so I need to finish his exam with zero eliminations. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.7.3">Therefore, I would like to arrange for there to be no eliminations in my class in the second half.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.8.1">Ichinose had sent that message to Ryuuen as soon as the special exam had begun. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.8.2">He read the message as soon as it came, and his reply came shortly after.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.9.1">“Well now, you’re being selfish. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.9.2">Do you really think I’m gonna just quietly go along with this?”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.10.1">“I think there’s room for negotiation. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.10.2">I’ll give you a present that will make you happy.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.11.1">“First things first: Do you think you can come out of Suzune’s attack unscathed?”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.12.1">For Ryuuen not to eliminate anyone from her class in the second half, she first had to get through the ten turns of the first half with zero eliminations.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.13.1">“I will.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.14.1">“Damn, someone’s confident. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.14.2">You haven’t been negotiating with Suzune already before talking to me, have you? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.14.3">Because if so, discussion’s over.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.15.1">A flimsy lie wasn’t going to work against someone cautious like Ryuuen. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.15.2">That being said, Ichinose had no intention of coordinating with Horikita. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.15.3">If she tried, her bargaining power would be reduced. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.15.4">Plus Sakayanagi’s class would make a move. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.15.5">That had to be avoided.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.16.1">“I want to protect everyone, and I don’t want anyone to get eliminated. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.16.2">My opponent knows that’s how I think. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.16.3">I’m sure that Horikita-san’s move will be to see if she can get five students in the range of eliminations. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.16.4">Then, she’ll want to verify if I’ll continue to protect those five.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.17.1">If she didn’t protect any of those people, then her opponent would assume that Ichinose was okay with getting an elimination, and by extension, that she was prepared for someone to be expelled if she came in last place. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.17.2">However, if Ichinose protected them, then it wouldn’t be so easy for Horikita, who attacked head-on. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.17.3">All of Ichinose’s precious protection slots would continue to be applied to those five students. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.17.4">Therefore, Horikita would change her plan to target students who haven’t made any mistakes, refrain from going after those students in range of elimination any further.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.18.1">“Unlike you and Sakayanagi-san, Ryuuen-kun, Horikita-san doesn’t want to get anyone expelled from the other classes. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.18.2">She just wants to win. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.18.3">She’ll attack the thirty-four other students that aren’t protected in a balanced way.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.19.1">Ichinose’s role in the first half of the exam was to create a situation in which she would use the protections at her disposal in the preliminary stages freely, deliberately allowing for five students who were not confident in the category to get in range of elimination, as a means of escape. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.19.2">It wasn’t going to be an easy fight, but it could be gamed.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.20.1">“So, if it plays out like you say, then I’ll do as you ask in the second half, and you get zero eliminations. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.20.2">What a happy ending for you. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.20.3">What’s in it for me?”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.21.1">“Twenty-five points, guaranteed. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.21.2">I’ll give you a bunch of names for people that we’re going to nominate. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.21.3">Of course, I’ll make sure to spread them out well so that the other classes don’t realize.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.22.1">If she told him in advance who she was going to attack, that alone would give him an advantage in the exam. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.22.2">She got a read receipt back on the message immediately, but it took Ryuuen about three minutes to respond. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.22.3">He’d been mulling it over.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.23.1">“Pass. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.23.2">Not that it’s a bad offer, but I got my own ideas.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.24.1">“I see. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.24.2">That’s too bad.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.25.1">Ichinose didn’t think that her proposal had been bad, but she had no choice but to let it go. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.25.2">If she made any further concessions in terms of the number of points, she would lose her chance for first place. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.25.3">More importantly, considering that Ryuuen didn’t even try to negotiate for more points, she had little chance of success.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.26.1">“Well then, guess that means I need to be careful…” said Ichinose.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.27.1">Negotiations had broken down. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.27.2">She could think of many ways to make the deal worse for her side, but she wasn’t going to do that. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.27.3">She would just have to fight on her own, going for zero eliminations, even if it were dangerous. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.27.4">However…</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.28.1">“You’re in luck.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.29.1">Ichinose received another message from someone she didn’t think she could count on.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.30.1">“What do you mean?”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.31.1">“If you can make sure that you get through the first half without getting anybody eliminated, I’ll take you up on half of your proposal.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.32.1">“Half?”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.33.1">“What I mean is I’ll agree not to eliminate anyone. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.33.2">But that part you said about twenty-five points guaranteed? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.33.3">That’s too much. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.33.4">If you make any weird moves in the first half, Sakayanagi will see right through you.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.34.1">“Okay then. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.34.2">What do you want from me?”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.35.1">“After we switch up the order of attacking and defending in the second half, accept points from me if you gotta. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.35.2">I’m not giving a detailed explanation. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.35.3">You’ll just have to decide whether or not to believe me.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.36.1">It was a rather puzzling offer, for him to offer her to be on the receiving end of points rather than have her give points. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.36.2">It was a story that another student would obviously think is a stupid joke, and that Ryuuen had no intention of negotiating from the very beginning.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.37.1">“I see…” mumbled Ichinose, quietly.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.38.1">Ichinose paused to think. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.38.2">Could she trust Ryuuen? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.38.3">It would’ve been a lie to say that Ichinose didn’t pause for as long as she could to consider it. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.38.4">Even so, Ichinose responded in less than a minute.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.39.1">“I understand. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.39.2">I believe you.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.40.1">No other student would have consented so quickly, but it wasn’t merely the decision of a soft heart. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.40.2">It was Ichinose’s philosophy. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.40.3">Her line of thinking. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.40.4">She intuited the significance of what Ryuuen was trying to do from his messages. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.40.5">The instant her initial message had been sent, it had been marked as read without even a second’s delay. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.40.6">Based on that, Ryuuen had his phone open to wait for her reply.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.41.1">In other words, there was something, if not exactly the same thing, that’d made Ryuuen want to work with her. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.41.2">They had this conversation right before the start of the special exam. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.41.3">After the second half had begun, the situation changed dramatically between turns eleven and fourteen. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.41.4">On turn fifteen, Sakayanagi’s class attacked Ryuuen’s class. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.41.5">The results were announced, and he had once again executed a perfect defense. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.41.6">Ichinose smiled to herself, stifling it just enough to avoid notice.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.42.1">“Amazing. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.42.2">So that’s what you were going for.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.43.1">“I’m gonna let you live, and you’re gonna be quiet.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.44.1">“You didn’t have to work with me, but you did. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.44.2">Thank you.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.45.1">“You think I did this outta the goodness of my heart? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.45.2">There ain’t nothing in it for me to have you come in last. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.45.3">I just took control of the score. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.45.4">That’s all.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.46.1">Ryuuen’s terms were contingent on Ichinose simply accepting points from him. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.46.2">If her class was losing to Sakayanagi’s, it would be simple for Ryuuen to pump up her score and force her into taking third place or higher. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.46.3">Ichinose, having foreseen the results of this special exam, was relieved that she wouldn’t be losing any friends. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.46.4">Immediately after they had been notified of the Unanimous Special Exam, Ichinose had chosen not to reallocate the Protect Point in their class, fearing the risk of sparking a conflict over this exam. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.46.5">Ichinose had almost regretted that decision. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.46.6">All that was left now was…</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.47.1">Karuizawa Kei currently had one question wrong. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.47.2">If Ichinose could get her to make one more mistake, she’d be within range of elimination. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.47.3">There was still a possibility that Class B could fall into last place. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.47.4">There were students in their class that had already been eliminated that were even lower in the pecking order than Karuizawa, though, so she couldn’t hope too strongly that she would be removed. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.47.5">Even so…there was a chance. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.47.6">To achieve it, the first step was to take a break from consecutive nominations once.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.48.1">“No… That’s a bad move…” said Ichinose. </span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.49.1">Ichinose had told herself that she should act for the sake of the class, not her own personal feelings. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.49.2">Ayanokouji hadn’t rejected her. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.49.3">Even if he continued his relationship with Karuizawa, he accepted her. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.49.4">In that case, there was a way she could clear a way for herself. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.49.5">While she felt that she was a lousy human being, at the same time, she could live with that.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.50.1">“Even if we don’t take first place, the real way to win is to make Sakayanagi-san come in last,” said Ichinose.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.51.1">Ichinose paused to catch her breath in the few minutes she had. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.51.2">Then, she looked at her phone. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.51.3">The meaning behind aiming for Karuizawa, no matter how many times she had been given protection up until this point—Ichinose was sure she’d gotten per point across. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.51.4">Having successfully restrained herself, Ichinose settled down once again.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.52.1">“I would like to nominate Karuizawa Kei-san, please.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.53.1">Turn sixteen was no different from the others; she nominated Karuizawa. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.53.2">With renewed determination, Ichinose didn’t hesitate. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.53.3">All she had to do now was hammer away at the same spot.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.54.1">“I would like to nominate Karuizawa Kei-san, please.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.55.1">Clutching her phone, Ichinose was convinced that, in truth, she was winning this special exam.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.56.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P__STAR__STAR__STAR__page_break"><span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.57.1">7.8</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.58.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span style=" font-weight: bold; font-size: 2.40em; letter-spacing: 0.60mm;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.59.1">A</span></span><span style=" font-size: 0.80em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.60.1">T THE START</span></span><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.61.1"> of turn fifteen, all four classes were finally neck and neck. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.61.2">Ichinose’s class was in first place with forty-two points, Horikita and Sakayanagi’s classes were tied for second with forty points, and Ryuuen’s class was in third with thirty-nine points. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.61.3">The surplus that Horikita’s class had accumulated in the first half had dried up. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.61.4">Ryuuen’s class still hadn’t taken the lead, but if things didn’t change, they would soon pull ahead at this rate. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.61.5">Although the students’ anxieties had been kept at bay by Ryuuen’s initial rocky start, dark, ominous clouds now began to gather overhead. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.61.6">Horikita’s class were now pushed into a situation where, depending on how things played out, they could very well come in last place. </span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.62.1">“You’re kidding me, right?! </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.62.2">This has to be a joke! </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.62.3">Seriously, give us a break!”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.63.1">“I absolutely do </span></span><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman__And__Italic"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.64.1">not </span></span><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.65.1">want to get expelled!”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.66.1">“Me, neither!”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.67.1">With one more person joining those who got eliminated in the first half, bringing the total to four, the students began to feel an impending sense of crisis, and went berserk. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.67.2">Even the other students were too distracted to cram. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.67.3">Horikita pulled out her chair and stood. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.67.4">It was time for her to start giving nominations, but she passed by the clamoring students with calm strides.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.68.1">“Don’t panic.”</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.69.1">Horikita, standing at the podium, directed those words at her classmates.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.70.1">“Yes, it’s true that this situation is close to being the worst-case scenario. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.70.2">There are currently four eliminations in our class. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.70.3">Ichinose-san has taken first place, and Ryuuen-kun’s class, which had been in last, is continuing to get perfect scores. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.70.4">They’re catching up with unusual speed. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.70.5">I can no longer say with absolute certainty that we will win,” announced Horikita.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.71.1">If Horikita could see through Ryuuen’s strategy and stop him from getting perfect scores, it would be a different story. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.71.2">But she couldn’t hope for that. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.71.3">She couldn’t even discern the source of Ichinose’s high hit rate.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.72.1">“All we can do is fight to the end,” said Horikita.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.73.1">Horikita could not give them a guarantee of victory. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.73.2">However, since this was a competitive exam anyway, the leader couldn’t even express what kind of uncertainty to expect. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.73.3">A leader couldn’t make weak statements or act self-assured for no reason. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.73.4">Her words rang with a truth that stuck in the minds of her classmates. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.73.5">Horikita was telling everyone to believe that she could overcome this. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.73.6">Even so, Yousuke, who normally echoed Horikita, was now only passively listening.</span></span></p>
</div>
</div>

    <img alt="Front Image1" class="insert" src="/hola-lector-agua/images/books/y2v10/OEBPS/Images/INTERIORIMAGES_07.jpg" loading="lazy" decoding="async"/>
  </section>

<div xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" id="book-columns"><div xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" id="book-inner">
<p id="auto_bookmark_toc_30" class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.1.1">“Believe in me,” said Horikita.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.2.1">Getting over the situation by a change in mentality. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.2.2">That was the only option left to her. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.2.3">However, looking at Horikita, I felt like she still had some fight left.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.3.1">Turn fifteen. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.3.2">Ichinose’s class’s attack.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.4.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span style=" font-weight: bold; font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.5.1">Successful protections by the defender: </span></span><span style=" font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.6.1">Karuizawa Kei, Satou Maya, Miyake Akito</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.7.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.8.1">Horikita had managed to pull off three successful protections, and the remaining two students had answered the question correctly, thereby earning a perfect score. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.8.2">Those five points had breathed life back into the class. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.8.3">However, the relentless chain of successive nominations had not been broken.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.9.1">“Wh-what the heck…?!” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.9.2">wailed Kei.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.10.1">Kei’s terror was winning out over her joy as she pulled away from the monitor. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.10.2">Even the classmates who didn’t know anything about the situation began to feel something unsettling about this unusual persistence. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.10.3">Horikita, who had succeeded in protecting Kei, didn’t seem to feel that way.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.11.1">The following turn, turn sixteen. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.11.2">Ichinose’s class’s attack.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.12.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span style=" font-weight: bold; font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.13.1">Successful protections by the defender: </span></span><span style=" font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.14.1">Karuizawa Kei, Nishimura Ryuuko</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.15.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.16.1">Two successful protections this time. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.16.2">But once again, Kei’s name was among them.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.17.1">“Stop it… What the hell…?” </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.17.2">muttered Kei.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.18.1">Ichinose nominated Karuizawa over and over again, repeating her attack endlessly. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.18.2">If you targeted just one particular student, it could make people think that it was a vicious attempt to get that student eliminated and thereby get them expelled. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.18.3">Such behavior, the kind that would destroy one’s image, continued.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.19.1">Turn seventeen. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.19.2">Ichinose’s class’s attack.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.20.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span style=" font-weight: bold; font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.21.1">Successful protections by the defender:</span></span><span style=" font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.22.1"> Karuizawa Kei, Hirata Yousuke</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.23.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.24.1">Even so, it didn’t stop.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.25.1">It just didn’t stop.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.26.1">No matter how many times she was defended.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.27.1">The nominations wouldn’t stop.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.28.1">“Why?! </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.28.2">Why am I the only one…? </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.28.3">I can’t believe this…” despaired Kei.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.29.1">Turn eighteen. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.29.2">Ichinose’s class’s attack.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.30.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span style=" font-weight: bold; font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.31.1">Successful protections by the defender: </span></span><span style=" font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.32.1">Karuizawa Kei, Hasebe Haruka, Onodera Kayano</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.33.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.34.1">Turn nineteen. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.34.2">Ichinose’s class’s attack.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.35.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span style=" font-weight: bold; font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.36.1">Successful protections by the defender:</span></span><span style=" font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.37.1"> Karuizawa Kei</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.38.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.39.1">Turn twenty. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.39.2">Ichinose’s class’s attack.</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.40.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span style=" font-weight: bold; font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.41.1">Successful protections by the defender: </span></span><span style=" font-size: 1.00em;"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.42.1">Karuizawa Kei, Sudou Ken</span></span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.43.1">&#160;</span></p>
<p class="P_Prose_Formatting"><span class="C_Current__And__Times_New_Roman"><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.44.1">Every turn in the second half. </span><span xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml" class="koboSpan" id="kobo.44.2">From start to finish, Ichinose had never removed Kei from her list of attack nominations, not even once.</span></span></p>
